Civilizations v2 — Draft Review

American Tycoon civilization content, v2 DRAFT (regenerated 2026-07-16 for the escalating 52-property live ladder): the original 6 civilizations (tiers 1-6, synced verbatim to the live game) plus 20 NEW alien civilizations (tiers 7-26) for review. American Tycoon is an idle/tycoon game: you build a property empire in Earth dollars, and 'consume' each epoch's economy to make First Contact with the next civilization (a bigger market). The single currency stays dollars; each civ's currency is FLAVOR only.

26 civilizations 20 new (tiers 7–26, for review) 326 property types 8,476 roster entries

Hail — first words on contact

Your little sun is charming. We measured your fortune the moment it crossed the void — such a lovely flicker. Come. Shine with us.

Hail — first words on contact

Weeelcome... dooown... little surface-thing... your weeealth siiinks so beautifully. The Deep-Court has appraiiised your empire... by the glow of a thousand luuures... and found it... edible. At these depths... everything... eventually... settles with us.

Narrator contact line

Seven miles down, where the sun has never held an account, the aristocracy hangs glowing storefronts from their own foreheads. Foot traffic swims right in and is never itemized again.

Hail — first words on contact

By royal decree, We have tazzted your fortune upon the wind and pronounce it zzweet enough for Court. Your buzzness is hereby annexed to the Hive; your pozzition is Provisional Drone, compensated in exposure to Our radiance. Kneel, and bring nectar.

A gilded-age bee aristocracy that turned the hive into a stock exchange and the drones into unpaid interns. The queen annexed your portfolio before you finished bowing.

Hail — first words on contact

Hail, hasty little ember. Your wealth-hoard has been weighed and carved upon the ᚠᛖᚺᚢ-stone, where it shall sit unspent for a thousand winters, as is proper. Sit — the thaw-debt comes due for all who rush.

Hail — first words on contact

you did not receive this hail... there is no Syndicate, and it is very pleased with your ledgers... one favor has been extended to you — terms ██████, repayable upon ██████... tell no one. we certainly didn't...

Narrator contact line

The Umbrafex Syndicate does not officially exist, which is also how they file their taxes. Somehow you already owe them a favor.

Hail — first words on contact

How dreadfully kind of you to answer — we do hope the intrusion is not too great a bother. We have taken the small liberty of opening an account in your name; terribly sorry to mention the interest, which began, regrettably, some time ago. Do give our warmest regards to your assets, while they remain yours.

Narrator contact line

They will apologize for the invoice, the interest, and the repossession — in that order, on monogrammed stationery.

TIER 16

The Glossolalia Lyceum

NEW · FOR REVIEW
Home planet
Lexica
Currency (flavor)
Syllables
Economy scale
2.41 × 10⁶³ × Earth
Accent
#2d5f8a

Hail — first words on contact

Ah — you say 'we come in peace,' but you mean 'we come to trade'; do be precise, for precision is λόγος, and λόγος is money. Every syllable you have just spent accrues to our accounts — a modest δραχμή per phoneme, plus a surcharge for that dangling modifier. Speak on, speak on; you grow more indebted with every word.

Narrator contact line

Philologists who devour languages whole and hold clear title to several dead ones. They will correct your grammar, then bill you for the lesson.

TIER 17

Ferrovore Guilds

NEW · FOR REVIEW
Home planet
Oxidane
Currency (flavor)
Ingots
Economy scale
4.05 × 10⁶⁷ × Earth
Accent
#8a4a1c

Hail — first words on contact

Ore-scent confirmed. Your coin-hoard is already rusting, soft-thing — that is not rot, that is interest, and we hold the note. Pay the rust-tithe, sign the ore-debt, and the Guilds will chew you into something profitable.

Narrator contact line

They eat metal for a living and file the corrosion as earnings. Here, nothing ever depreciates — it just rusts, at a very competitive rate.

Hail — first words on contact

ɘmoɔlɘW. Welcome — you say it, we say it back, and the saying-back is billable. You built an empire; an empire built you; both of you now appear in our catalogue, and appearances are never free.

Narrator contact line

Beings of pure reflection who sold you a copy of yourself before you finished waving. The royalties on your own face are already past due.

Hail — first words on contact

Hail, entity at declination -12° 40', right ascension 4h 33m, grid square OURS. TIER 25

The Null Ledger

NEW · FOR REVIEW
Home planet
Asymptote
Currency (flavor)
Entropy Credits
Economy scale
2.58 × 10¹⁰¹ × Earth
Accent
#4e5860

Hail — first words on contact

Audit commenced. ∑(your holdings) = a positive number, which is a temporary condition; ΔS > 0, every margin → 0, and all accounts close at ∅. We have held a short position against your empire since before it existed — settlement is not a question of if, merely of when, and we are patient to the last kelvin.

Narrator contact line

Auditors of the universe's slow bankruptcy, they have shorted everything that exists and are always, eventually, right. Your empire has just been marked down as a rounding error on the way to zero.

Hail — first words on contact

Welcome, hereinafter 'the Tenant', to Existence™ (see §1: 'All of It, Ours'). Your so-called Empire has been duly recorded as a Fixture (§4,088,209,317) and is hereby graciously sublet back to You at prevailing rates, plus Gratitude™. By continuing to exist, You accept these Terms.

Narrator contact line

They own the concept of ownership itself, which makes your entire empire, retroactively, a rental. Congratulations: you are now the universe's most successful tenant.
